Figure 11.
The
LAST
\star
configuration language: syntax and semantics.

Functional calculi such as
LAST
\star
feature a clear distinction between the static and dynamic parts of their languages.
That is, a
LAST
\star
program starts as a closed functions (the static part) and evolves into several threads operating in parallel and communicating through message passing (the dynamic part).
We refer to the static and dynamic parts of
LAST
\star
as terms and configurations, respectively.
In general, one writes a
LAST
\star
program as a single main thread containing a term that forks and connects child threads.
To contrast, process calculi such as
APCP
blur the lines between such static and dynamic parts, as
APCP
“programs” are immediately written as configurations of parallel subprocesses connected on channels.

Figure

11
gives the configuration syntax for
LAST
\star
.
Given a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
, the configuration
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\phi\,M}
denotes a corresponding thread, where the marker
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\phi}
is useful to distinguish the main thread
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}}
from child threads
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}}
—this distinction will be useful for typing.
Buffered channels are denoted
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}x[\vec{m}\rangle y)C}
.
Here,
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}C}
has access to the endpoints
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}y}
.
The buffer itself
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x[\vec{m}\rangle y}
is an ordered sequence of messages (values and labels, denoted
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\vec{m}}
) sent on
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}
and to be received on
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}y}
.
This means that
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}C}
may send/select on
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}
and receive/offer on
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}y}
.
Once the buffer is empty (i.e.,
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\vec{m}}={\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\epsilon}
),
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}y}
may switch roles.
Configuration
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}C\parallel D}
denotes the parallel composition of
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}C}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}D}
.

Figure

11
(top) also defines thread contexts
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathpzc{F}}
as term reduction contexts inside threads, and configuration contexts
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathpzc{G}}
.

The reduction semantics for configurations is defined on specific arrangements of buffers and threads.
To ensure such arrangements, we define a structural congruence for configurations (
\equiv_{\mathtt{C}}
), the least congruence on configurations satisfying the rules in
Figure

11
(center).
Rule [
sc-res-swap
] swaps the direction of buffered channels and thus the input/output roles of the channel’s endpoints.
Rule [
sc-res-comm
] defines commutativity of buffered channels.
Rule [
sc-res-ext
] defines scope extrusion/inclusion for buffered channels.
Rules [
sc-par-comm
] and [
sc-par-assoc
] define commutativity and associativity for parallel composition.

Figure

11
(bottom) gives the reduction semantics for configurations (
\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}
).
It defines how terms in threads interact with each other by exchanging messages through buffered channels.
Rule [
red-new
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}}
construct in a thread by creating a new buffered channel and returning the endpoints
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}y}
.
Rule [
red-fork
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}}
construct in a thread by creating a new child thread.
Rule [
red-send
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}}
by placing the value at the end of the enclosing buffer and returning the endpoint.
Dually, Rule [
red-recv
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}}
by retrieving the value at the start of the enclosing buffer and returning it along with the endpoint.
Rule [
red-select
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{select}}}
by placing the label at the end of the enclosing buffer and returning the endpoint.
Dually, Rule [
red-case
] reduces a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{case}}}
by retrieving the label at the start of the enclosing buffer and applying the label’s corresponding continuation to the endpoint.
Rule [
red-res-nil
] garbage collects buffers that are no longer used, and Rule [
red-par-nil
] garbage collects child threads that have reduced to unit.
Rules [
red-lift-C
] and [
red-lift-M
] close configuration reduction under configuration contexts and enable terms in threads to reduce, respectively.
Rule [
red-conf-lift-SC
] closes configuration reduction under structural congruence.

{exa}

[The Bookshop Scenario, Revisited]

We illustrate the message-passing behavior of
LAST
\star
by considering the bookshop example from
Section

2.2
.
Note that
LAST
\star
does not have
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{close}}}
constructs: we will motivate them in
Section

5
; here, for simplicity, we consider the system in
Section

2.2
without these constructs.

First, let us explore how
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathsf{Sys}}
sets up some channels and threads:

\displaystyle\hphantom{{}\mathbin{\rightarrow}_\mathtt{C}{}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(s,s^{\prime})=\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Shop}(s);\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(m,m^{\prime})=\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(m);\mathsf{Son}(s^{\prime},m^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(s,s^{\prime})=(y,y^{\prime})\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Shop}(s);\\
\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(m,m^{\prime})=\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(m);\mathsf{Son}(s^{\prime},m^{\prime})\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Shop}(y);\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(m,m^{\prime})=\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(m);\mathsf{Son}(y^{\prime},m^{\prime})\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(m,m^{\prime})=\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(m);\mathsf{Son}(y^{\prime},m^{\prime})\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(m,m^{\prime})=(z,z^{\prime})\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(m);\mathsf{Son}(y^{\prime},m^{\prime})\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}\mathsf{Mother}(z);\mathsf{Son}(y^{\prime},z^{\prime})\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathsf{Son}(y^{\prime},z^{\prime})\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

Next, let us see how the son sends the book title and his choice to buy to the shop, and then his connection with the shop to his mother, without waiting for any of his messages to be received:

\displaystyle\equiv_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\epsilon\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s^{\prime}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}\text{``Dune''}\,y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s^{\prime}_{1}=y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s^{\prime}_{2}=\mathinner{\mathtt{select}}\mathsf{buy}\,y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s^{\prime}_{2}=y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}m^{\prime}_{1}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}y^{\prime}\,z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z^{\prime}[y^{\prime}\rangle z)\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}m^{\prime}_{1}=z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z^{\prime}[y^{\prime}\rangle z)\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Mother}(z)\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)}

Now, we can see how the mother receives the shop’s connection and sends her credit card information:

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z^{\prime}[y^{\prime}\rangle z)\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(x,m_{1})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(x,m_{1})=(y^{\prime},z)\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}x_{1}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}\text{visa}\,y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa},\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}x_{1}=y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa},\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},x_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathsf{Shop}(y)\big)\end{array}}

Finally, the sequence of reductions in
Figure

12
shows how the shop reads messages and sends the book, and how the mother forwards it to her son.

\displaystyle\hphantom{{}={}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa},\mathsf{buy},\text{``Dune''}\rangle y)\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}(\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big(\begin{array}[t]{@{}l@{}}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},x_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\end{array}\\
\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{title},s_{1})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}y\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\end{array}}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa},\mathsf{buy}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{title},s_{1})=(\text{``Dune''},y)\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa},\mathsf{buy}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{case}}y\mathinner{\mathtt{of}}\{\ldots\}\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,(\lambda s_{2}\ldots)~y\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y^{\prime}[\text{visa}\rangle y)\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{card},s_{3})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}y\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{card},s_{3})=(\text{visa},y)\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s_{4}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\,y\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}s_{4}=y\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\ldots\big)\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},x_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}y^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},x_{2})=(\text{book}(\text{``Dune''}),y^{\prime})\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\ldots\big)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}y[\epsilon\rangle y^{\prime})\big((\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}m_{2}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\,z\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}()\big)\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}m_{2}=\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\,z\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}m_{2}=z\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\ldots\parallel{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\lozenge}\,()\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}z^{\prime}\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}(\textit{book},m^{\prime}_{2})=(\text{book}(\text{``Dune''}),z^{\prime})\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}\textit{book}\big)}

\displaystyle\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}(\bm{\nu}z[\epsilon\rangle z^{\prime})\big({\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})\big)}\mathbin{\rightarrow}_{\mathtt{C}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}{\mathchoice{\scriptstyle}{\scriptstyle}{\scriptscriptstyle}{}\blacklozenge}\,\text{book}(\text{``Dune''})}

Figure 12.

Reduction sequence from
Section

4.1
.
Recall that notation
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\text{book}(\textit{title})}
is syntactic sugar for a lookup function.
We abbreviate unchanged threads using “
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\ldots}
”.

4.2.
The Type System of
LAST
\star

The type system for
LAST
\star
includes functional types for functions and pairs and session types for message passing.
The syntax and meaning of functional types (
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T},{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}U}
) and session types (
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S}
) are as follows:

\displaystyle\begin{array}[t]{@{}r@{}lr@{\kern.5ex}l@{\kern 2ex}lr@{\kern.5ex}l@{\kern 2ex}lr@{\kern.5ex}l@{\kern 2ex}lr@{}}{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T},{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}U}::=&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T\times U}&\text{pair}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T\mathbin{\multimap}U}&\text{function}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\bm{1}}&\text{unit}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S}&\text{session}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S}::=&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{!}T.S}&\text{send}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{?}T.S}&\text{receive}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{\oplus}\{i:T\}_{i\in I}}&\text{select}\kern 2.15277pt&\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}\hfil\kern 8.61108pt&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\&\{i:T\}_{i\in I}}&\text{branch}\\
~~\mathbin{\mbox{\large{$\mid$}}}&{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{end}}\end{array}

Session type duality (
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\overline{S}}
) is defined as usual; note that only the continuations, and not the messages, of send and receive types are dualized.

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\overline{{!}T.S}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{?}T.\overline{S}}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\overline{{\oplus}\{i:S_{i}\}_{i\in I}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\&\{i:\overline{S_{i}}\}_{i\in I}}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\overline{\mathsf{end}}}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{end}}

Figure 13.
LAST
\star
typing rules for terms (top), buffers (center), and configurations (bottom).

The type system for
LAST
\star
has three layers: typing for terms, for buffers, and for configurations.
Typing for terms uses judgments of the form

{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Gamma}\vdash_{\mathtt{M}}{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}:{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}

which decrees that
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
has a behavior described by
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}
using the typing context
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Gamma}
, which is defined as a list of variable-type assignments
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}x}:{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}
.

Figure

13
(top) gives the typing rules for terms.
Rules often combine typing contexts
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Gamma}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Delta}
to form
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Gamma},{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Delta}
; this implicitly assumes that the domains of
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Gamma}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\Delta}
are disjoint. We briefly comment on them:

•

Rules [
typ-var
], [
typ-abs
], [
typ-app
], [
typ-unit
], [
typ-pair
], and [
typ-split
] are standard typing rules for functional terms.

•

Rule [
typ-new
] types the
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{new}}}
construct as a pair of dual session types.

•

Rule [
typ-fork
] takes a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\bm{1}}
and a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}N}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}
to type the
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{fork}}}
construct as
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}
; as we will see in the typing of configurations, child threads must be typed
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\bm{1}}
, which explains the type of
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
.

•

Rule [
typ-end
] allows weakening typing contexts with
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{end}}
-typed variables, as closed sessions are not used.

•

Rule [
typ-send
] takes a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T}
and a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}N}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{!}T.S}
to type a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{send}}}
of
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
along
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}N}
as the continuation type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S}
.
Dually, Rule [
typ-recv
] takes a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{?}T.S}
to type a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{recv}}}
along
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
as a pair of the message’s payload type and continuation
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}T\times S}
.

•

Rule [
typ-sel
] takes a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{\oplus}\{i:S_{i}\}_{i\in I}}
to type the selection of some
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}j}\in I
along
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
as the continuation
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S_{j}}
.
Dually, Rule [
typ-case
] takes a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
of type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\&\{i:S_{i}\}_{i\in I}}
and for every
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}i}\in I
a term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}N_{i}}
typed
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S_{i}\mathbin{\multimap}U}
(i.e., a function from the label
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}i}
’s continuation type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S_{i}}
to some common but arbitrary type
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}U}
) to type a
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{case}}}
along
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}M}
as
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}U}
.

{exa}

To illustrate the typing rules, let us derive the typing of term
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathsf{Son}(s^{\prime},m^{\prime})}
from
Section

2.2
.
As in
Section

4.1
, we omit the
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{close}}}
construct.
To type the sugared terms
{\color[rgb]{0.0977,0,0.543}\mathinner{\mathtt{let}}x=M\mathinner{\mathtt{in}}N}
we use a sugared Rule [
typ-let
] derivable from Rules [
typ-abs
] and [
typ-app
].
We consider
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{Str}}
(string),
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{B}}
(book), and
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}\mathsf{P}}
(payment) to be primitive non-linear types that can be weakened/contracted at will and are self dual.
Below,
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S}={\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{!}\mathsf{P}.{?}\mathsf{B}.\mathsf{end}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}S^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0.7422,0.2891,0}{?}\mathsf{B}.\mathsf{end}}
.
We omit “
typ-
” from rule labels.

\displaystyle\pi\mathrel{\triangleq}\begin{array}[b]{@{}l@{}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower \displaystyle\begin{array}[b]{@{}l@{}}\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\ignorespaces\lower
